# Deadpool & Wolverine

This article is about the 2024 film. For the comic book series, see [*Wolverine and Deadpool*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wolverine_and_Deadpool).

***Deadpool & Wolverine*** is a 2024 American [superhero film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Superhero_film) based on [Marvel Comics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marvel_Comics) featuring the characters [Deadpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool) and [Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wolverine_\(character\)). Produced by [Marvel Studios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marvel_Studios), [Maximum Effort](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maximum_Effort), and [21 Laps Entertainment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/21_Laps_Entertainment), and distributed by [Walt Disney Studios Motion Pictures](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walt_Disney_Studios_Motion_Pictures), it is [the 34th film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Marvel_Cinematic_Universe_films) in the [Marvel Cinematic Universe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marvel_Cinematic_Universe) (MCU) and the sequel to *[Deadpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool_\(film\))* (2016) and *[Deadpool 2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool_2)* (2018). The film was directed by [Shawn Levy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shawn_Levy), who wrote the screenplay with [Ryan Reynolds](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ryan_Reynolds), [Rhett Reese](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rhett_Reese), [Paul Wernick](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_Wernick), and [Zeb Wells](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zeb_Wells). Reynolds and [Hugh Jackman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hugh_Jackman) respectively star as [Wade Wilson / Deadpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wade_Wilson_\(film_character\)) and [Logan / Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logan_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), alongside [Emma Corrin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emma_Corrin), [Morena Baccarin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morena_Baccarin), [Rob Delaney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rob_Delaney), [Leslie Uggams](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leslie_Uggams), [Aaron Stanford](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aaron_Stanford), and [Matthew Macfadyen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matthew_Macfadyen). In the film, Deadpool works with a reluctant Wolverine from another universe to stop the [Time Variance Authority](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_Variance_Authority_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) (TVA) from destroying his own universe.

Development on a third *Deadpool* film began at [20th Century Fox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/20th_Century_Fox) by November 2016, but was moved to Marvel Studios when [Fox was acquired by Disney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Acquisition_of_21st_Century_Fox_by_Disney) in March 2019. [Wendy Molyneux and Lizzie Molyneux-Logelin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Molyneux_sisters) joined in November 2020 as writers. Levy was hired to direct in March 2022, when Reese and Wernick returned from the previous films for rewrites. The creative team had difficulty settling on a story until Jackman decided to reprise his role as Wolverine from Fox's [*X-Men* film series](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men_\(film_series\)) in August 2022. Several other actors from the *X-Men* films and other Marvel productions also returned as part of a [multiverse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multiverse_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) story, which serves as a tribute to Fox's Marvel films. Filming began in May 2023, taking place at [Pinewood Studios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pinewood_Studios), [Bovingdon Studios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bovingdon_Studios), and [Norfolk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Norfolk) in England as well as Los Angeles. Production was suspended in July due to the [2023 SAG-AFTRA strike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_SAG-AFTRA_strike). Filming resumed in November and [wrapped](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wrap_\(filmmaking\)) in January 2024. The title was revealed a month later. The [film's soundtrack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool_&_Wolverine_\(soundtrack\)) features an original score by [Rob Simonsen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rob_Simonsen) and numerous existing songs, including [Madonna](142056.md)'s "[Like a Prayer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Like_a_Prayer_\(song\))" for key sequences. *Deadpool & Wolverine* is the first [R-rated](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/R_rating_\(Motion_Picture_Association\)) MCU film, retaining that rating from the prior *Deadpool* films.

*Deadpool & Wolverine* premiered on July 22, 2024, at the [David H. Koch Theater](1587430.md) in New York City, and was released in the United States on July 26 as part of [Phase Five](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marvel_Cinematic_Universe:_Phase_Five) of the MCU. Critics praised the performances of Reynolds and Jackman as well as the humor, but were less positive about the film overall. It was a commercial success, grossing \$1.338 billion worldwide and becoming the [second-highest-grossing film of 2024](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_in_film#Highest-grossing_films), the [highest-grossing R-rated film](60598574.md), and the [20th-highest-grossing film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_highest-grossing_films) at the time. With a net production budget of \$429 million, *Deadpool & Wolverine* is believed to be [one of the most expensive films ever made](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_most_expensive_films).[^cite_note-Budget-2] It received various accolades. A sequel is in development.

## Plot

In 2018, [Wade Wilson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wade_Wilson_\(film_character\)) uses [Cable](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cable_\(character\))'s time travel device to prevent the death of his girlfriend, [Vanessa Carlysle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vanessa_Carlysle_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)). He then travels from his universe, [Earth-10005](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Earth-10005),[^cite_note-5] to [Earth-616](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Earth-616_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\))—also called the "[Sacred Timeline](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sacred_Timeline)"—hoping to join that reality's [Avengers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avengers_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)).[^cite_note-6] He is rejected by [Happy Hogan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Happy_Hogan_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) and returns to his own universe. Six years later, Wade has broken up with Vanessa, retired from being the masked mercenary Deadpool, and works as a used-car salesman with his friend and former [X-Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Force) member [Peter Wisdom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Wisdom_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)).

During Wade's birthday party, he is detained by the [Time Variance Authority](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_Variance_Authority_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) (TVA) and brought to [Mr. Paradox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paradox_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), who explains that they are an organization outside of time that monitors the Sacred Timeline and the wider [multiverse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multiverse_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)). He offers Wade a future on the Sacred Timeline which Wade accepts, until Paradox reveals that Wade's universe is deteriorating as a result of the death of its stabilizing "[anchor being](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anchor_being)", [Logan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logan_\(film_character\)).[^cite_note-Logan-7] Paradox plans to use a "Time Ripper" device to accelerate this process. Wade steals Paradox's [TemPad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TemPad) device and uses it to travel to Logan's grave, hoping to resurrect him and save their timeline. When this fails, Wade travels the multiverse searching for a replacement "[variant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Variant_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe_term\))" of Logan.

Wade returns to the TVA with a [variant of Logan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logan_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)). Paradox says an anchor being cannot be replaced, especially by this variant who is considered to be the worst [Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wolverine_\(character\)) in the multiverse. When Wade deduces that Paradox is acting without the knowledge of his superiors, Paradox sends Wade and Logan to the [Void](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Void_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe_location\)), a wasteland at the end of time. Wade claims the TVA can change events in Logan's past, convincing him to work together to escape the Void. The pair are captured alongside [Johnny Storm](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Johnny_Storm_\(film_character\)) and taken to [Cassandra Nova](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cassandra_Nova_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), the powerful and sadistic twin sister of [X-Men](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) leader [Charles Xavier](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Xavier_\(film_character\)). Cassandra, who made a deal with the TVA to oversee the Void, kills Johnny and leaves Wade and Logan to be consumed by the creature [Alioth](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alioth_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), but the pair escape.

Logan and Wade meet Deadpool variants "Nicepool" and "[Dogpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dogpool)", who direct them towards a [resistance group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Resistance_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) that has been fighting Cassandra. Wade accidentally admits that he is not certain whether the TVA can change Logan's timeline, and the pair fight until they are both unconscious. They are found by the resistance group, consisting of [Laura](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Laura_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), [Elektra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elektra_Natchios_\(film_character\)), [Blade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blade_\(New_Line_franchise_character\)), and [Gambit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Remy_LeBeau_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)). Wade proposes an alliance and Laura convinces Logan—regretful of his past actions which led to the deaths of his fellow X-Men—to join. The resistance members distract Cassandra's henchmen while Wade and Logan block her powers using [Juggernaut](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cain_Marko_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\))'s helmet. Cassandra is betrayed by her follower [Pyro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Allerdyce_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) on behalf of Paradox and almost dies, until Logan convinces Wade to remove the helmet. Cassandra heals herself and uses a [Sling Ring](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sling_Ring) to open a portal to Earth-10005 for Logan and Wade.

Cassandra learns about the Time Ripper from Pyro, kills him, and follows Logan and Wade to Earth-10005. She plans to use the Time Ripper to destroy all timelines, leaving only the Void. Cassandra summons an [army of Deadpool variants](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool_Corps_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), who kill Nicepool and battle Wade and Logan until Peter arrives to distract them. Paradox tells Wade and Logan that one of them could destroy the Time Ripper by disrupting its power flow, but this would kill them. Wade and Logan destroy the Time Ripper together, killing Cassandra in the process, and survive by sharing the burden. Paradox is arrested by the TVA's [Hunter B-15](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hunter_B-15), who congratulates Wade and Logan and says their actions have stopped Earth-10005 from deteriorating. Wade asks Hunter B-15 to save the resistance group from the Void and change the history of Logan's world. She explains that the latter is not possible because Logan's history is what led to him being a hero now, but allows Logan to stay in Wade's universe. During a gathering with Wade's friends that includes Dogpool and a rescued Laura, Logan encourages Wade to reconcile with Vanessa.

## Cast

Picture of the film's cast and crew together on a stage in 2024

Producer Kevin Feige, star Ryan Reynolds, director Shawn Levy, and co-stars Hugh Jackman, Emma Corrin, Dafne Keen, Jennifer Garner, Chris Evans, Channing Tatum and Wesley Snipes promoting the film at San Diego Comic-Con in July 2024

- [Ryan Reynolds](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ryan_Reynolds) as [Wade Wilson / Deadpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wade_Wilson_\(film_character\)):\
  A wisecracking mercenary from [Earth-10005](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Earth-10005) with accelerated healing but severe scarring over his body after undergoing an experimental regenerative [mutation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mutant_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) to treat terminal cancer.

  [^cite_note-MolyneuxSisters-8]

  &#x20;Wade begins the film retired from being Deadpool, no longer dating Vanessa Carlysle, and working as a used car salesman.

  [^cite_note-EWCoverJun2024-9]

  &#x20;Writer [Rhett Reese](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rhett_Reese) described Deadpool as a [fish-out-of water](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/fish%20out%20of%20water), a lunatic dropped into the "sane world" of the [Marvel Cinematic Universe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marvel_Cinematic_Universe) (MCU).

  [^cite_note-ReeseJun2022-10]

  &#x20;Though Reynolds said the audience did not have to take Deadpool seriously, particularly with his [fourth wall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fourth_wall) breaking and [unreliable narration](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Unreliable_narrator), he wanted them to be invested in the other characters.

  [^cite_note-VanityFairJun2024-11]

  &#x20;Deadpool's opening dance number was performed by Nick Pauley, who is credited as "Dancepool".

  [^cite_note-IGNEasterEggs-12]

  [^cite_note-DeadpoolVariants/Film-13]

  &#x20;Reynolds also portrays the alternate universe "[variant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Variant_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe_term\))" Nicepool,

  [^cite_note-15]

  &#x20;who has no scars or healing factor, for which he is credited as "Gordon Reynolds".

  [^cite_note-IGN35Minutes-4]

  [^cite_note-DeadpoolVariantsGR+-16]

- [Hugh Jackman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hugh_Jackman) as [Logan / Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logan_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)):\
  A mutant and former member of the [X-Men](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) with healing abilities, retractable claws, and an [adamantium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adamantium_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\))-infused skeleton.

  [^cite_note-ReynoldsJackmanRelease-17]

  &#x20;This Wolverine is a variant from a universe that he let down.

  [^cite_note-EWCoverJun2024-9]

  [^cite_note-OfficialTrailerCameos-18]

  &#x20;This, and the pairing with Deadpool, allowed Jackman to explore new aspects of the character from his appearances as a [different version of Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logan_\(film_character\)) in [20th Century Fox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/20th_Century_Fox)'s [*X-Men* film series](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men_\(film_series\)).

  [^cite_note-VanityFairJun2024-11]

  [^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Wolverine-19]

  &#x20;Jackman said Logan and Wade are opposites and have a "quarreling dynamic",

  [^cite_note-JackmanDec2022Playlist-20]

  &#x20;while director [Shawn Levy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shawn_Levy) said their relationship is affected by both characters being "haunted with regret".

  [^cite_note-VanityFairJun2024-11]

  &#x20;They chose to depict the character's backstory, including the deaths of his fellow X-Men, through sound design only to keep the focus on Wolverine's regrets rather than on the appearances of other characters.

  [^cite_note-NoXMenDeaths-21]

  &#x20;To prepare for the role, Jackman began a vigorous workout routine that included eating up to 8,000 calories a day.

  [^cite_note-JackmanWorkout-22]

  &#x20;Jackman also portrays several other Wolverine variants: one possessing the character's comic book-accurate height of 5 feet 3 inches (160 cm), with actor Luke Bennett serving as [body double](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Body_double);

  [^cite_note-Bennett-23]

  &#x20;Wolverine's gambling alter-ego [Patch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patch_\(character\)), who wears an eyepatch and white tuxedo; an [Old Man Logan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Man_Logan) variant; a variant being crucified on a giant X, inspired by the cover of *[Uncanny X-Men](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uncanny_X-Men)* #251 (July 1989); a variant wearing a brown costume who fights the [Hulk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hulk_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), referencing the cover of *[The Incredible Hulk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Incredible_Hulk_\(comic_book\))* #340 (October 1987); and an *[Age of Apocalypse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Age_of_Apocalypse)* (1995) variant with [glam rock](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glam_rock) hair, one hand, and a black-and-red costume.

  [^cite_note-WolverineVariants-24]

- [Emma Corrin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emma_Corrin) as [Cassandra Nova](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cassandra_Nova_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)):\
  A mutant with [telekinetic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Telekinetic) and [telepathic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Telepathic) powers who is the twin sister of [Charles Xavier](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Xavier_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)). She rules over the [Void](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Void_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe_location\)), a wasteland at the end of time.

  [^cite_note-EWMay2024-25]

  [^cite_note-NerdistVoidCameos-26]

  &#x20;Having not played many villains before, Corrin was excited to take on the role and was inspired by [Christoph Waltz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christoph_Waltz)'s character [Hans Landa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hans_Landa) from the film *[Inglourious Basterds](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Inglourious_Basterds)* (2009).

  [^cite_note-CorrinEmpire-27]

  [^cite_note-CorrinHB-28]

  &#x20;Reynolds and Levy explained Cassandra to Corrin as someone who would endear others to the point that "you are going to be best friends for life", only to realize she could easily kill them.

  [^cite_note-EWCoverJun2024-9]

  &#x20;Corrin was interested in having a physical transformation for the role but was told by the studio that this was not required for the character.

  [^cite_note-CorrinNoPT-29]

  &#x20;Corrin also wanted to pay homage to [Patrick Stewart](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patrick_Stewart) and [James McAvoy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_McAvoy)'s performances as Xavier in previous *X-Men* films, and to be faithful to Cassandra's relationship with Xavier in the comics.

  [^cite_note-CorrinSR-30]

- [Morena Baccarin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morena_Baccarin) as [Vanessa Carlysle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vanessa_Carlysle_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)): Wade's former fiancée.

  [^cite_note-BaccarinApr2023-31]

  [^cite_note-SRExplained-32]

  &#x20;Baccarin noted that she has a smaller role compared to the previous *Deadpool* films.

  [^cite_note-BaccarinNov2024-33]

- [Rob Delaney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rob_Delaney) as [Peter Wisdom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Wisdom_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)): A member of Wade's [X-Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Force) team who works with him at the car dealership. Peter encourages Wade to return to his life as Deadpool.

  [^cite_note-PeterNerdist-34]

- [Leslie Uggams](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leslie_Uggams) as [Blind Al](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blind_Al_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)): Wade's blind, elderly roommate

  [^cite_note-SoniUggams-35]

- [Aaron Stanford](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aaron_Stanford) as [John Allerdyce / Pyro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Allerdyce_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)): A [pyrokinetic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pyrokinetic) mutant working for Cassandra.

  [^cite_note-EWMay2024-25]

  [^cite_note-PolygonSuperBowl-36]

  &#x20;Stanford wears a more comics-accurate costume than he did in Fox's *X-Men* films.

  [^cite_note-EWCoverJun2024-9]

- [Matthew Macfadyen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matthew_Macfadyen) as [Mr. Paradox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paradox_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)):\
  An agent of the [Time Variance Authority](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_Variance_Authority_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) (TVA), an organization outside of time and space that monitors the [multiverse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multiverse_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)). Paradox plans to speed up the death of Earth-10005 with a "Time Ripper" machine.

  [^cite_note-IGN35Minutes-4]

  &#x20;Macfadyen said the character was untrustworthy but not a villain, though he thought Paradox would prefer to be a supervillain or superhero rather than being stuck doing "a mundane thing of monitoring the timelines in the multiverse".

  [^cite_note-MacfadyenJul2024-37]

"daywalker" [Eric Brooks / Blade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blade_\(New_Line_franchise_character\)) from [New Line Cinema](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Line_Cinema)'s [*Blade* film ## Production

### Background

After the success of *[Deadpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool_\(film\))* (2016), [20th Century Fox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/20th_Century_Fox) began developing two sequels. The third film was set to include the superhero team [X-Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Force).[^cite_note-UniversePlans-59] *Deadpool* director [Tim Miller](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tim_Miller_\(director\)) chose not to return for the sequels due to creative differences with star [Ryan Reynolds](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ryan_Reynolds), and [David Leitch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Leitch) was hired to direct *[Deadpool 2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool_2)* (2018) in November 2016. Fox was looking for another filmmaker to develop the third film.[^cite_note-LeitchDP2-60] In March 2017, *Deadpool* and *Deadpool 2* co-writer [Rhett Reese](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rhett_Reese) said X-Force would be introduced in *Deadpool 2* before starring in a planned spin-off film that would launch "something bigger", separate from the third *Deadpool* film, which would be more personal.[^cite_note-ReeseMar2017-61] When the [acquisition of 21st Century Fox by Disney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Acquisition_of_21st_Century_Fox_by_Disney) was announced in December 2017, [the Walt Disney Company](37398.md) CEO [Bob Iger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bob_Iger) said Reynolds's [Wade Wilson / Deadpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wade_Wilson_\(film_character\)) would be integrated with the [Marvel Cinematic Universe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marvel_Cinematic_Universe) (MCU). This was despite those films being rated [PG-13](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PG-13_\(Motion_Picture_Association\)) and the *Deadpool* films being [R-rated](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/R_rating_\(Motion_Picture_Association\)).[^cite_note-DisneyFoxMCU-62] Iger was willing to make R-rated *Deadpool* films "as long as we let the audiences know what's coming" and said a "Marvel-R brand" could be created for characters like Deadpool.[^cite_note-DisneyMarvelR-63] In May 2018, Reynolds said a third *Deadpool* film may not be made due to the shift of focus to X-Force.[^cite_note-ReynoldsDP3Unsure-64] Reese and his writing partner [Paul Wernick](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_Wernick) said a third film would happen after Reynolds took a break from the character and the X-Force film was released, similar to the MCU crossover film *[The Avengers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Avengers_\(2012_film\))* (2012) being released between *[Iron Man 2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iron_Man_2)* (2010) and *[Iron Man 3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iron_Man_3)* (2013).[^cite_note-ReeseWernickMay2018IGN-65]

At the end of May 2018, Leitch expressed interest in returning for another *Deadpool* film, depending on the schedule.[^cite_note-LeitchDoGMay2018-66] A third *Deadpool* film was believed to be in active development by that August, with production planned to take place in [Atlanta](3138.md), Georgia, rather than in [Vancouver](32706.md), Canada, where the previous films were made.[^cite_note-Aug2018AtlantaProd-67] *[Once Upon a Deadpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Once_Upon_a_Deadpool)*, a PG-13 version of *Deadpool 2*, was released at the end of the year. It was watched carefully by Disney and [Marvel Studios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marvel_Studios) to see how it might inform their approach to the character in the MCU.[^cite_note-OnceUponADeadpool-68] While promoting *Once Upon a Deadpool*, Reynolds confirmed that a third *Deadpool* film was in development and said it would go in a "completely different direction".[^cite_note-OnceUponADeadpool2-69] Reynolds later revealed that they were considering a [road trip film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Road_trip_film) styled after *[Rashomon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rashomon)* (1950) featuring [Hugh Jackman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hugh_Jackman)'s [*X-Men* film series](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men_\(film_series\)) character [Logan / Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logan_\(film_character\)).[^cite_note-ReynoldsRoadTrip-70] [Karan Soni](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karan_Soni), who portrayed [Dopinder](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dopinder) in the first two *Deadpool* films, reiterated these plans and explained that the film would have focused on Deadpool trying to save Christmas by going on a road trip to the North Pole.[^cite_note-SoniInitialDP3Plans-71] Jackman had himself envisioned a team-up film with Reynolds inspired by *[48 Hrs.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/48_Hrs.)* (1982) while attending a screening of *Deadpool* in 2016, but chose to retire from the role of Wolverine after *[Logan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logan_\(film\))* (2017).[^cite_note-JackmanOct2022-72] In March 2019, Disney officially acquired Fox and gained the film rights to several [Marvel Comics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marvel_Comics) characters for Marvel Studios, including [Deadpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool) and the [X-Men](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men).[^cite_note-DisneyFoxDealClose-73][^cite_note-FoxDisneyPressRelease-74] The Marvel-based films that Fox had been developing were placed "on hold".[^cite_note-FoxMarvelOnHold-75]

[Josh Brolin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Josh_Brolin), who portrayed [Cable](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cable_\(character\)) in *Deadpool 2* and [Thanos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thanos_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) in the MCU, said in June 2019 that he had discussed his future as Cable with Marvel Studios.[^cite_note-BrolinJun2019-76] In October, [Zazie Beetz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zazie_Beetz) said she would be surprised if she did not reprise her *Deadpool 2* role of [Domino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Domino_\(character\)) in a future film.[^cite_note-BeetzOct2019-77] That month, Reese and Wernick said they and Reynolds were taking a "much-needed rest from Deadpool" while they waited to hear from Marvel Studios about the franchise's future. They confirmed that another *Deadpool* film would be made, that it would be R-rated like the previous films, and that it would integrate the character with the MCU. Wernick said there was never a set [pitch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pitch_\(filmmaking\)) for a third *Deadpool* film between their exhaustion following *Deadpool 2* and the announcement of Fox's acquisition by Disney. Reese said they had to "land on the right idea and once we do, I think we'll be off to the races".[^cite_note-ReeseWernickOct2019-78]

### Development

Picture of actor Ryan Reynolds in 2024

Ryan Reynolds promoting the film at San Diego Comic-Con in July 2024

Reynolds announced that he was meeting with Marvel Studios in October 2019.[^cite_note-ReynoldsOct2019-79] At that time, Marvel Studios president [Kevin Feige](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kevin_Feige) was unsure how to integrate Deadpool, the [X-Men](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), and [mutants](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mutant_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) in general into the MCU. He described Reynolds as an "idea machine" who pitched many different approaches for the film,[^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Pitch-80] including a low-budget road trip film with Deadpool and Dopinder that was compared to [independent films](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Independent_film),[^cite_note-EWCoverJun2024-9] and one that included a dance number with Wolverine set to [Elton John](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elton_John)'s "[I Guess That's Why They Call It the Blues](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/I_Guess_That's_Why_They_Call_It_the_Blues)".[^cite_note-MusicPitch-81] Reynolds also pitched his previous idea for a *Rashomon*–style team-up with Wolverine that would tell one story from three different perspectives,[^cite_note-EWCoverJun2024-9][^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Pitch-80] but was told it would not be possible to include Wolverine.[^cite_note-JackmanOct2022-72][^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Pitch-80] In December, Reynolds said the "whole team" was actively developing a *Deadpool* film at Marvel Studios.[^cite_note-ReynoldsDec2019-82]

Reynolds and Marvel Studios met with potential writers throughout October 2020, including [Wendy Molyneux and Lizzie Molyneux-Logelin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Molyneux_sisters). Their pitch aligned with Reynolds and Marvel's intentions for the film, and the pair were hired the next month. Reynolds and the studio were open to Leitch returning as director,[^cite_note-MolyneuxSisters-8] and he met with them about it, but scheduling prevented him from returning.[^cite_note-LeitchAug2022-83] While filming *[The Adam Project](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Adam_Project)* (2022) with director [Shawn Levy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shawn_Levy), Reynolds asked Levy about working together on the next *Deadpool* film and Levy was enthusiastic about the idea.[^cite_note-VanityFairJun2024-11] In January 2021, Feige said Reynolds was supervising the writing process and his busy schedule meant filming would not begin until after 2021.[^cite_note-FeigeJan2021-84] An idea Reynolds had in 2020 or 2021 was for an action sequence set to the song "[Like a Prayer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Like_a_Prayer_\(song\))" by [Madonna](142056.md). It was intended to follow the death of [Piotr Rasputin / Colossus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piotr_Rasputin_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) which would have motivated Deadpool after being at a low point.[^cite_note-ReynoldsLevyAug2024Collider-85] In July, Reynolds and [Taika Waititi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Taika_Waititi) promoted their film *[Free Guy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_Guy)* (2021), also directed by Levy, as their respective Marvel characters Deadpool and [Korg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Korg_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) in a commercial titled *[Deadpool and Korg React](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool_and_Korg_React)*. Deadpool, who often breaks the [fourth wall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fourth_wall), discusses joining the MCU with Korg in the commercial.[^cite_note-FreeGuyAdVariety-86][^cite_note-FreeGuyAdDS-87] The next month, Feige said filming would begin in 2022 and Marvel Studios had a release timeframe in mind.[^cite_note-FeigeAug2021-88] In November, Beetz and [Rob Delaney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rob_Delaney) expressed interest in returning as their *Deadpool 2* characters Domino and [Peter Wisdom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Wisdom_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)).[^cite_note-BeetzNov2021-89][^cite_note-DelaneyNov2021-90] Beetz ultimately did not return.[^cite_note-NoBeetz-91]

Levy was revealed to be directing the film in March 2022,[^cite_note-LevyMar2022THR-92][^cite_note-LevyMar2022Variety-93] and said filming would not begin until after 2022 due to his busy schedule through the remainder of the year.[^cite_note-LevyNo2022Filming-94] Reese and Wernick were hired to rewrite the screenplay,[^cite_note-LevyMar2022THR-92][^cite_note-LevyMar2022Variety-93] and said they were excited to use MCU elements. They added that Disney and Marvel were supportive of Deadpool making [meta](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/meta) jokes about the MCU because of the success of the previous *Deadpool* films.[^cite_note-ReeseWernickJun2022-95] In June, [Raymond Chan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ray_Chan_\(art_director\)) was revealed to be the production designer after working on the Marvel Studios miniseries *[The Falcon and the Winter Soldier](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Falcon_and_the_Winter_Soldier)* (2021).[^cite_note-Chan-96] A month later, Feige said the film would be elevated from the first two in a similar way to other third installments of MCU film series, such as *[Captain America: Civil War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Captain_America:_Civil_War)* (2016), *[Thor: Ragnarok](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thor:_Ragnarok)* (2017), and *[Avengers: Infinity War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avengers:_Infinity_War)* (2018).[^cite_note-FeigeJuly2022-97]

Picture of actor Hugh Jackman in 2024

Hugh Jackman promoting the film at San Diego Comic-Con in July 2024. Jackman's decision to return as Wolverine from the *X-Men* films, reversing his plans to retire as the character, solved the creative team's issues with settling on a story.

Levy and Reynolds worked on the script with Reese, Wernick, and [Zeb Wells](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zeb_Wells),[^cite_note-VanityFairJun2024-11] who previously worked on the Marvel Studios miniseries *[She-Hulk: Attorney at Law](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/She-Hulk:_Attorney_at_Law)* (2022) and animated series *[Marvel Zombies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marvel_Zombies_\(TV_series\))* (2025), and the film *[The Marvels](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Marvels)* (2023).[^cite_note-Disney+DayDeadline-98][^cite_note-Wells-99] They all came to feel that they did not have an original story that was not derivative of the other *Deadpool* films. Levy said they felt the film had to be "deserving" of being the first *Deadpool* film set within the MCU, while still feeling "grounded because this is an earthbound, gritty, realistic superhero franchise". Levy and Reynolds were prepared to suggest to Feige that they could not move forward at the time because of the trouble they had landing on a story idea,[^cite_note-VanityFairJun2024-11] and executive producer Wendy Jacobson said Marvel was also struggling to settle on a core idea for the story.[^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Pitch-80] In August 2022, Jackman reached out to Reynolds about wanting to be part of the film. Reynolds was preparing for a meeting with Feige to discuss how to proceed given their lack of story ideas, and realized that including Wolverine would solve many of the issues they were coming up against.[^cite_note-VanityFairJun2024-11][^cite_note-JackmanOct2022-72] Jackman had a change of heart, after being content with his decision to retire as Wolverine for several years, and decided that a team-up with Reynolds "could be so much fun; I'll probably have more fun on that movie than anything I've ever done".[^cite_note-JackmanDec2022Playlist-20][^cite_note-JackmanOct2022-72] Feige advised Jackman not to return as he felt Wolverine's death in *Logan* was the "greatest ending in history" and did not want to undo that. He was convinced by the idea that Jackman would portray a [new version of Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logan_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) from an alternate universe within the [multiverse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multiverse_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)). Jackman confirmed that he wanted to return after taking a long drive to think about it.[^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Wolverine-19]

In September 2022, Reynolds announced that Jackman would return as Wolverine for *Deadpool 3*, which was set for release on September 6, 2024.[^cite_note-ReynoldsJackmanRelease-17] Alongside Feige and *X-Men* franchise producer [Lauren Shuler Donner](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lauren_Shuler_Donner),[^cite_note-Corrin-100][^cite_note-TrailerViews-101] Reynolds and Levy were producing through their respective production companies [Maximum Effort](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maximum_Effort) and [21 Laps Entertainment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/21_Laps_Entertainment).[^cite_note-MaximumEffort-102][^cite_note-21Laps-103] [Simon Kinberg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Simon_Kinberg) was an executive producer after producing the previous *Deadpool* films.[^cite_note-Kinberg-104][^cite_note-DPWolverineMarvel-105] He said the new film had been authored by Levy, Reynolds, and Feige, and they all cared deeply about *Logan*, Deadpool, and Wolverine.[^cite_note-KinbergSep2023-106] Reynolds and Jackman said they would avoid affecting the events of *Logan* through the multiverse story.[^cite_note-JackmanDifferentLogan-107][^cite_note-JackmanDec2022Variety-108] Levy said preserving *Logan*'s legacy was top priority for the creative team,[^cite_note-VanityFairJun2024-11] and added that the legacies of Deadpool and Wolverine informed the story and themes.[^cite_note-EWCoverJun2024-9] Jackman reached out to *Logan* director [James Mangold](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Mangold) to inform him of the story and said Mangold was both enthusiastic about the idea and relieved that *Logan* would not be impacted.[^cite_note-JackmanDec2022Playlist-20] Jackman said the film may not be titled *Deadpool 3*,[^cite_note-JackmanOct2022-72] with himself, Reynolds, and Levy referring to it at different times as "The Deadpool/Wolverine film",[^cite_note-ReynoldsNov2022-109] *Wolverine and Deadpool*,[^cite_note-JackmanJan2023-110] *Deadpool Versus Wolverine*, *Deadpool and Wolverine*, and *Deadpool 3 with Wolverine*.[^cite_note-NoTitle-111] The title *Deadpool & Friend*, which Reynolds claimed was the official title, leaked online in February 2024. After a negative fan response, a different title was officially announced the next day: *Deadpool & Wolverine*.[^cite_note-TitleLeakChange-112][^cite_note-EWSuperBowl-113] Despite being the third *Deadpool* film, Levy did not want to call it *Deadpool 3* because it is a "two-hander character adventure" that focuses on both characters.[^cite_note-LevyTitleApr2024-114] Reynolds said it was still a *Deadpool* film in terms of tone, with violence, swearing, and "meta mayhem". He called it "the most Deadpool movie in the history of Deadpool".[^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Deadpool-115]

The film's budget was initially reported to be \$200 million.[^cite_note-VarietyBudget-116] Tax returns filed by Disney revealed in 2025 that the "final costs exceeded the production budget", with a gross cost of \$533.7 million and a net cost of \$429 million due to tax credits. It is believed to be [one of the most expensive films of all time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_most_expensive_films). The increased costs came following disruptions to production caused by the [2023 Hollywood labor disputes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Hollywood_labor_disputes).[^cite_note-Budget-2]

### Pre-production

Pre-production work began by October 2022,[^cite_note-BackstageOct2022-117] less than two months after Jackman decided to join. Jacobson said this was one of the fastest turnarounds she had seen for a big production.[^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Pitch-80] The release date was pushed back to November 8, 2024, to accommodate other MCU film delays.[^cite_note-Nov2024Release-118] In December, Levy said filming was set to begin in May 2023.[^cite_note-LevyDec2022-119] Reynolds lobbied Marvel Studios to film in Vancouver, where he is from and the previous *Deadpool* films were made, but conceded that "they have their \[established] infrastructure, and you just have to sort of fall in line".[^cite_note-ReynoldsVancouverFilmingHope-120] Filming was set to take place in [London](17867.md) by February 2023.[^cite_note-LondonFilming-121]

[Patrick Stewart](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patrick_Stewart), who portrayed [Charles Xavier / Professor X](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Xavier_\(film_character\)) in the *X-Men* film series and reprised the role in the MCU film *[Doctor Strange in the Multiverse of Madness](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Doctor_Strange_in_the_Multiverse_of_Madness)* (2022), said in February that he had been approached about potentially making an appearance in the film,[^cite_note-Stewart-122] but Levy later denied that there were ever any plans for Stewart to appear.[^cite_note-LevyStewartDeny-123] Also that month, [Emma Corrin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emma_Corrin) joined the cast in a lead villain role. Marvel had been considering Corrin since late 2022,[^cite_note-Corrin-100] after Levy saw them in a stage production of [Virginia Woolf](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virginia_Woolf)'s *[Orlando](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Orlando:_A_Biography)*,[^cite_note-CorrinEmpire-27] but there were scheduling issues that had to be worked through before Corrin signed on.[^cite_note-Corrin-100] In March, the [Time Variance Authority](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_Variance_Authority_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) (TVA) and other elements from the MCU television series *[Loki](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Loki_\(TV_series\))* (2021–2023) were reported to be appearing.[^cite_note-TVAReport-124] [Matthew Macfadyen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matthew_Macfadyen) joined the cast in an undisclosed role,[^cite_note-Macfadyen-125] which was described as a [third wheel](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/third%20wheel) to Deadpool and Wolverine.[^cite_note-Macfadyen2-126] Leading up to the start of filming, several cast members were confirmed to be reprising their roles from the previous *Deadpool* films: Karan Soni as Dopinder, [Leslie Uggams](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leslie_Uggams) as [Blind Al](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blind_Al_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)),[^cite_note-SoniUggams-35] [Morena Baccarin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morena_Baccarin) as [Vanessa Carlysle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vanessa_Carlysle_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), [Stefan Kapičić](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stefan_Kapi%C4%8Di%C4%87) as the voice of Piotr Rasputin / Colossus,[^cite_note-BaccarinKapičić-39] Rob Delaney as Peter Wisdom,[^cite_note-Delaney-127] [Brianna Hildebrand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brianna_Hildebrand) as [Negasonic Teenage Warhead](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Negasonic_Teenage_Warhead_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), and [Shioli Kutsuna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shioli_Kutsuna) as [Yukio](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yukio_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)).[^cite_note-HildebrandKutsuna-38]

Levy said developing the film was one of the most fun creative experiences of his career, citing its self-awareness, violence, and foul language.[^cite_note-LevyDec2022-119] He did not want to alter the franchise's DNA and called the new film "raw, audacious, \[and] very much R-rated", comparing it to the [buddy comedy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Buddy_film) films *48 Hrs.*, *[Planes, Trains and Automobiles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Planes,_Trains_and_Automobiles)* (1987), and *[Midnight Run](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Midnight_Run)* (1988).[^cite_note-LevyDeadlineSep2023-128] Soni said it was "taking full advantage of the MCU" and would feature jokes about Feige.[^cite_note-SoniJokes-129] Levy said there was one line that Disney asked to be changed,[^cite_note-LevyAug2024EW-130] which was revealed when a version of the script was published in anticipation of [awards season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Film_awards_seasons): "Disney is so cheap. I can barely breathe with all this [Mickey Mouse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mickey_Mouse) cock in my throat" was changed to "Fuck, *now* Disney gets cheap? It's like [Pinocchio](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pinocchio) jammed his face in my ass and started lying like crazy."[^cite_note-DisneyLineChange-131]

Picture of Ryan Reynolds in costume as Deadpool and Hugh Jackman in costume as Wolverine in an outdoor location in 2023

Ryan Reynolds as Deadpool (left) and Hugh Jackman as Wolverine (right) during filming. Reynolds's costume was updated from the previous *Deadpool* films, while Jackman wore a comic-accurate Wolverine costume for the first time.

Graham Churchyard and [Mayes C. Rubeo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mayes_C._Rubeo) were the costume designers, both returning from previous MCU films.[^cite_note-ChurchyardEW-132][^cite_note-RubeoDeadline-133] Jackman wears a yellow and blue costume similar to Wolverine's costumes from *[X-Men: The Animated Series](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men:_The_Animated_Series)* (1992–1997) and the comic books,[^cite_note-JackmanCostumeTHR-134][^cite_note-JackmanCostumeEW-135] specifically [*Astonishing X-Men* volume 3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Astonishing_X-Men#Volume_3_\(2004%E2%80%932013\)) (2004–2013) drawn by [John Cassaday](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Cassaday);[^cite_note-JackmanCostumeAstonishing-136] it also features elements of the character's 1970s design by [John Romita Sr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Romita_Sr.) and [Dave Cockrum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dave_Cockrum) and the 1990s design by [Jim Lee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jim_Lee).[^cite_note-JackmanCostumeNewsarama-137] Jackman did not wear a comics-accurate costume in the *X-Men* film series, though it was referenced in *[X-Men](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men_\(film\))* (2000) and a [deleted scene](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deleted_scene) from *[The Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Wolverine_\(film\))* (2013).[^cite_note-JackmanCostumeTHR-134] The film was therefore an opportunity to see Jackman in a comics-accurate costume for the first time,[^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Wolverine-19] and Levy felt this could be the last chance for Jackman to wear such a costume. They went through several iterations and fittings to get the suit right.[^cite_note-LevyEWWolverineSuit-138] Jackman said putting the suit on "looked so right \[and] felt so right" and he questioned why he had not worn such a costume in his previous films.[^cite_note-EmpireMay2024Wolverine-19] An early reveal of the costume was met with positive reactions online,[^cite_note-JackmanCostumeTotalFilm-139][^cite_note-JackmanCostume/Film-140][^cite_note-JackmanCostumePolygon-141] and led some commentators to determine that Jackman was portraying a different iteration of the character from the one he portrayed in the *X-Men* films.[^cite_note-JackmanCostume/Film-140][^cite_note-JackmanCostumeVariety-142] Reynolds also has an updated costume from the previous *Deadpool* films,[^cite_note-JackmanCostumeEW-135] featuring a brighter shade of red and larger black circles on his mask. This is reminiscent of the character's comic book design from the 1990s.[^cite_note-ReynoldsNewCostumeCBR-143] Levy was pleased with the "overwhelmingly positive" response to the suit designs.[^cite_note-LevyEWWolverineSuit-138]

### Filming

[Principal photography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Principal_photography) began on May 22, 2023, taking place in London and at [Pinewood Studios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pinewood_Studios) in Buckinghamshire, England,[^cite_note-KapičićFilmingStart-144][^cite_note-THRFilmingStart-145][^cite_note-PinewoodStudios-146] under the [working title](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Working_title) *Tidal Wave*.[^cite_note-WorkingTitle-147] [George Richmond](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Richmond_\(cinematographer\)) served as the cinematographer after working with Levy and Reynolds on *Free Guy*,[^cite_note-Richmond-148] while George Cottle was the supervising stunt coordinator and second unit director.[^cite_note-CottleMPA-149] Levy worked with Marvel Studios and the producers of the [Netflix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Netflix) series *[Stranger Things](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stranger_Things)* (2016–2025), on which he was an executive producer, so their schedules would allow him to direct episodes of that series' [final season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stranger_Things_season_5) since filming for the two projects was expected to overlap.[^cite_note-StrangerThingsConflicts-150][^cite_note-StrangerThingsStrikeDelay-151] The production was not expected to be impacted by the [2023 Writers Guild of America strike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Writers_Guild_of_America_strike) that began in early May, outside of Reynolds being unable to contribute to the script during filming as he had done for the previous films. Marvel Studios was reportedly planning to shoot what they could during principal photography and make any necessary writing adjustments during already scheduled [reshoots](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pick-up_\(filmmaking\)).[^cite_note-ReynoldsWritersStrike-152] Location filming was prioritized over soundstages where possible, because the filmmakers felt this gave the film a more grounded feeling.[^cite_note-LevyDeadlineSep2023-128][^cite_note-LevyEWWolverineSuit-138][^cite_note-ReynoldsSetPhotosResponse-153] In mid-June, the release date was moved forward to May 3, 2024.[^cite_note-May2024Release-154]

Filming for a car crash took place in London in early July 2023.[^cite_note-LondonSetPhotos-155] Reynolds and Jackman filmed scenes in [Norfolk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Norfolk) soon after.[^cite_note-NorfolkFilming-156] Production was suspended on July 14 because of the [2023 SAG-AFTRA strike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_SAG-AFTRA_strike).[^cite_note-SAGAFTRAStrike-157] 35 days of filming were completed before the shutdown, with another 35 days left.[^cite_note-LevyDeadlineSep2023-128][^cite_note-LevyColliderSep2023-158] Baccarin completed all of her scenes prior to the strike.[^cite_note-BaccarinFilming-159] Filming was set to resume following the conclusion of the [dual Hollywood strikes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Hollywood_labor_disputes),[^cite_note-LevyDeadlineSep2023-128] and Disney paid to keep the sets intact during the hiatus.[^cite_note-Nov2023DelayReason-160] Levy and Reynolds began work on the editing and visual effects,[^cite_note-LevyTheWrapOct2023-161][^cite_note-Nov2023DelayReason-160] though they had not prioritized filming major visual effects sequences before the strike.[^cite_note-LevyColliderSep2023-158] Levy compared one of the filmed sequences to the [lightsaber](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lightsaber) duel between [Luke Skywalker](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luke_Skywalker) and [Darth Vader](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Darth_Vader) in the film *[Return of the Jedi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Return_of_the_Jedi)* (1983), and had asked the stunt team to look to that scene's blocking, tempo, and [framing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Film_frame).[^cite_note-RotJInspiration-162] Cottle was allowed to give input on the editing and direction.[^cite_note-CottleMPA-149] Another key action sequence is the one that Reynolds wanted to be set to Madonna's "Like a Prayer", which had evolved to include Wolverine alongside Deadpool.[^cite_note-ReynoldsLevyAug2024Collider-85] Reynolds and Levy met with the singer to ask for permission to use the song, which she gave along with a suggestion that it be combined with a [gospel choir](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gospel_choir) for the film.[^cite_note-MadonnaNote-163]

The film, along with others that were mid-way through production at the start of the strikes, was said to be a top priority for the studios to resume production following the conclusion of the SAG-AFTRA strike.[^cite_note-FilmingResumePriority-164] In October 2023, Levy questioned if the film would make its May 2024 release date given the strike was still ongoing at the time,[^cite_note-LevyTheWrapOct2023-161] shortly before it was reported that it was likely to be delayed even if filming resumed by the start of 2024.[^cite_note-NoMay2024Release-165] After the conclusion of the SAG-AFTRA strike on November 9, filming was expected to resume in London within the following two weeks, before [Thanksgiving](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thanksgiving_\(United_States\)).[^cite_note-Nov2023DelayReason-160][^cite_note-ThanksgivingFilmingRestart-166] Reynolds announced that the character [Dogpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dogpool_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) would appear,[^cite_note-Dogpool-167] played by [dog actor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dog_actor) [Peggy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peggy_\(dog\)),[^cite_note-DogpoolPeggy-168] and the release date was pushed back to July 26, 2024.[^cite_note-Jul2024Release-169] Filming resumed by November 23.[^cite_note-FilmingRestart-170] Levy said his time editing and reviewing footage during the strikes allowed him to gain a better understanding of the film, and he was able to return to work feeling focused and not rushed.[^cite_note-LevyAPApr2024-171] Reynolds's [Wrexham A.F.C.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wrexham_A.F.C.) co-chairman [Rob McElhenney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rob_McElhenney) visited the set to film a [cameo appearance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cameo_appearance) as a TVA agent and discuss the football club. His visit was captured by the *[Welcome to Wrexham](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Welcome_to_Wrexham)* (2022–present) documentary series,[^cite_note-McElhenney-172][^cite_note-McElhenney2-173] though his scene was ultimately cut from the film.[^cite_note-McElhenneyRoleCut-174] At the end of December, [Bona Film Group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bona_Film_Group) signed a deal with [TSG Entertainment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TSG_Entertainment) to invest in a slate of films, including the *Deadpool* franchise;[^cite_note-BonaTSGInvest-175] TSG Entertainment and [20th Century Studios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/20th_Century_Studios) both receive "in association with" credits on the film.[^cite_note-VarietyReview-176][^cite_note-PressKit-177]<sup>: 3 </sup> Principal photography, which also took place at [Bovingdon Film Studios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bovingdon_Film_Studios),[^cite_note-Bovingdon-178] [wrapped](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wrap_\(filmmaking\)) on January 24.[^cite_note-FilmingWrap-179] One day of filming then took place in Los Angeles to film [Jon Favreau](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jon_Favreau)'s appearance as [Happy Hogan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Happy_Hogan_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), reprising his role from previous MCU films, and to film the opening dance sequence with choreographer Nick Pauley.[^cite_note-ReynoldsLevyAug2024Collider-85] A [United States Copyright Office](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_Copyright_Office) filing for the film indicated that some filming occurred in Vancouver as well.[^cite_note-CopyrightOffice-180]

During filming, [Jennifer Garner](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jennifer_Garner) was reported to be appearing as [Elektra Natchios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elektra_Natchios_\(film_character\)) after portraying a version of that character in the Fox-produced film *[Daredevil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daredevil_\(film\))* (2003) and its spin-off *[Elektra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elektra_\(2005_film\))* (2005).[^cite_note-Garner-43] Garner initially denied her involvement.[^cite_note-GarnerDeny-181] Set photos revealed that other characters from previous Marvel films, including [Sabretooth](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victor_Creed_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) and [Toad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mortimer_Toynbee_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), would also be appearing,[^cite_note-SetPhotosSabretooth-182][^cite_note-SetPhotosToad-183] along with a large 20th Century Fox logo set piece.[^cite_note-ReynoldsSetPhotosResponse-153] *[Variety](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Variety_\(magazine\))* reported that the film was taking a similar approach to characters from prior Fox-produced Marvel films as the MCU film *[Spider-Man: No Way Home](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spider-Man:_No_Way_Home)* (2021), which features multiple characters from [past *Spider-Man* films](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spider-Man_in_film) coming together.[^cite_note-VarietyReturningCharacters-184] Levy acknowledged some of the casting rumors surrounding the film, including suggestions that [Taylor Swift](5422144.md) would be making a cameo appearance as [Dazzler](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dazzler_\(Marvel_Comics\)), but would not confirm which rumors were true;[^cite_note-LevyEWWolverineSuit-138][^cite_note-LevyTheWrapOct2023-161] Swift does not appear,[^cite_note-NoSwift-185][^cite_note-NoSwiftReynolds-186] and Levy later said there were never any plans for her to appear.[^cite_note-LevyAug2024Variety-187] Regarding the characters that do appear—including [Aaron Stanford](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aaron_Stanford) reprising his role as [John Allerdyce / Pyro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Allerdyce_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) from the films *[X2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X2_\(film\))* (2003) and *[X-Men: The Last Stand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X-Men:_The_Last_Stand)* (2006)—Levy said they were added as the story developed and were not forced into the film based on a wishlist of cameos.[^cite_note-EWMay2024-25] Commenting on the set photo leaks, Reynolds said they created a difficult situation for the production and he hoped websites would stop publishing unfinished, out-of-context leaked images.[^cite_note-ReynoldsSetPhotosResponse-153] He noted that the franchise is associated with leaks since the first *Deadpool* film was only able to be made following [a leak of its original test footage](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deadpool_\(film\)#Development).[^cite_note-ReynoldsFakeLeaks-188] Levy said the presence of paparazzi on set was a "price we're comfortable paying" in exchange for filming on location.[^cite_note-LevySetPhotosResponse-189]

### Post-production

The film's copyright filing in February 2024 confirmed Garner's role and revealed Corrin to be playing [Cassandra Nova](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cassandra_Nova_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), the twin sister of Charles Xavier.[^cite_note-CopyrightOffice-180] The writers initially chose the comic book character [Mephisto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mephisto_\(Marvel_Comics\)), whose introduction to the MCU had been the subject of fan speculation, as the main antagonist. This changed when Jackman suggested using Cassandra due to Wolverine's relationship with Xavier.[^cite_note-Mephisto-190] A series of trailers were released in the following months, revealing more character details: Macfadyen as TVA agent [Mr. Paradox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paradox_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\));[^cite_note-MacfadyenParadox-191] Randal Reeder and [Lewis Tan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lewis_Tan) reprising their respective roles as Buck and [Rusty / Shatterstar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shatterstar_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) from the previous *Deadpool* films;[^cite_note-GizmodoTeaserEEsSlide2-192][^cite_note-GizmodoTeaserEEsSlide3-193] the return of Stanford as Pyro and [Tyler Mane](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tyler_Mane) as Sabretooth from the *X-Men* films;[^cite_note-PolygonSuperBowl-36][^cite_note-SabretoothTrailer-41] and the inclusion of the creature [Alioth](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alioth_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) from *Loki*.[^cite_note-OfficialTrailerCameos-18] [Vinnie Jones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vinnie_Jones) and [Jason Flemyng](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jason_Flemyng) were asked to reprise their respective *X-Men* film roles of [Cain Marko / Juggernaut](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Juggernaut_\(character\)) and [Azazel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Azazel_\(Marvel_Comics\)), but deals could not be agreed upon.[^cite_note-Jones-194][^cite_note-Flemyng-195] [Terry Crews](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terry_Crews) had talks about returning as [Bedlam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bedlam_\(comics\)) from *Deadpool 2*, but these did not go anywhere.[^cite_note-Crews-196]

[Dean Zimmerman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dean_Zimmerman_\(film_editor\)), a frequent collaborator with Levy, edited the film with [Shane Reid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shane_Reid).[^cite_note-EditorsMPA-197] Levy said they were busy finishing it at the end of April 2024.[^cite_note-LevyAPApr2024-171] Early screenings took place by then,[^cite_note-LevyTestsApr2024-198] with high test scores being given to both Deadpool and Wolverine as well as their chemistry.[^cite_note-EditorsMPA-197] The ending originally immediately revealed that Deadpool and Wolverine had survived their ordeal, until Reynolds's wife [Blake Lively](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blake_Lively) suggested their fate be left unknown for a moment to add suspense. The updated scene, including Pardox's impassioned speech about the heroes' seeming sacrifice, was filmed during a day-and-a-half of reshoots. Levy said Lively's suggestion made the ending "so much more satisfying".[^cite_note-Reshoots-199] Lively and some of her and Reynolds's children have voice cameos as variants of Deadpool, which Levy attributed to them being available for recording.[^cite_note-LevyAug2024Variety-187] *The Adam Project* co-star [Walker Scobell](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walker_Scobell) was originally set to voice the variant [Kidpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kidpool_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)), but during production his voice changed too much due to puberty and he was replaced by Reynolds's daughter Inez,[^cite_note-ScobellKidpool-200] with Scobell instead voicing an unseen Deadpool variant who yells "Go back to Canada, asshole" in a scene that was cut from the film.[^cite_note-WalkerScobellCameo-201][^cite_note-WalkerScobellCameo2-202] Reynolds originally provided the voice for the variant [Cowboypool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cowboypool) before [Matthew McConaughey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matthew_McConaughey) agreed to voice the character.[^cite_note-ReynoldsCowboypool-203] Jokes about the cast were cleared with them before inclusion, including a scripted joke about Garner's divorce with [Ben Affleck](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ben_Affleck) and an improvised joke about Jackman's divorce.[^cite_note-LevyAug2024Variety-187] Post-production was completed on June 19.[^cite_note-PostProductionComplete-204] Despite the multiverse story and connections to other projects, Levy did not expect audiences to do homework before watching the film and intended for it to be an entertaining experience on its own.[^cite_note-LevyAPApr2024-171] Reynolds said they did not want to just set-up future projects and Marvel never pressured them into doing so, only asking them to make "one really satisfying self-contained story".[^cite_note-ReynoldsLevyAug2024Collider-85]

In July, Favreau and [Wunmi Mosaku](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wunmi_Mosaku) were revealed to be reprising their MCU roles as Happy Hogan and the TVA's [Hunter B-15](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hunter_B-15).[^cite_note-IGN35Minutes-4][^cite_note-Mosaku-44] The writers planned to have [Robert Downey, Jr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Downey,_Jr.) reprise his MCU role as [Tony Stark / Iron Man](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tony_Stark_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) alongside Favreau, but he turned them down after reading the scene. Reese and Wernick said this was the only time an actor turned down a role in the film.[^cite_note-ReeseWernickAug2024IW-205] Also in July, a final trailer revealed that [Dafne Keen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dafne_Keen) was returning as [Laura / X-23](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Laura_\(Marvel_Cinematic_Universe\)) from *Logan*. Before this, Keen had denied her involvement in the film while promoting the *[Star Wars](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Star_Wars)* series *[The Acolyte](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Acolyte_\(TV_series\))* (2024).[^cite_note-KeenEW-42] Marvel chose to reveal her role in the trailer after Keen asked to attend the premiere.[^cite_note-KeenVariety-206] Keen enjoyed keeping her role a secret, taking inspiration from [Andrew Garfield](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrew_Garfield)'s denials about appearing as [Peter Parker / Spider-Man](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Parker_\(The_Amazing_Spider-Man_film_series\)) in *No Way Home* before that film's release.[^cite_note-KeenEW-42]
